Let's express using factorials. P(A - q +1, K) = (A - q +1)! / (A - q +1 - K)!.
P(A - q, K-1) = (A - q)! / (A - q - K +1)!.
Note A - K = unused after batch. Let B = A - K. Then:
P(A-q+1,K) = (A-q+1)! / (B - q +1)!.
K*P(A-q,K-1) = K * (A-q)! / (B - q)!.
So new[q] = dp[q] * (A-q+1)!/(B-q+1)! + K * (A-q)!/(B-q)! * suffix[q+1].

But the update formula for T_j is linear and might be optimized. We have M up to 2e5. We need to apply for each interval k an update to all T_j for j≥k:
T_j <- a_{k,j} T_j + (A_k - a_{k,j}) Total_{k-1}, where a_{k,j}=P(R_k? R=R_{k-1}, C_j, Δ_k) = (R_{k-1}-C_j)!/(R_k-C_j)!.
Then Total_k = T_k (after update).
This is a triangular linear system. We can unroll T_k:
T_k = Σ_{h=1}^k Total_{h-1} * (A_h - a_{h,k}) * ∏_{t=h+1}^k a_{t,k}.
The product of a_{t,k} telescopes: a_{t,k} = (R_{t-1}-C_k)!/(R_t-C_k)!. Product t=h+1..k = (R_h-C_k)!/(R_k-C_k)!.
A_h = P(R_{h-1}, Δ_h) = R_{h-1}!/R_h!.
Thus Total_k = Σ_{h=1}^k Total_{h-1} [ (R_{h-1}!/R_h! - (R_{h-1}-C_k)!/(R_k-C_k)!) * (R_h-C_k)!/(R_k-C_k)! ]
= Σ Total_{h-1} [ R_{h-1}! (R_h-C_k)!/(R_h! (R_k-C_k)!) - (R_{h-1}-C_k)!/(R_k-C_k)! ]
= 1/(R_k-C_k)! Σ_{h=1}^k Total_{h-1} [ R_{h-1}! * (R_h-C_k)!/R_h! - (R_{h-1}-C_k)! ].
This matches earlier. Let D_h = Total_{h-1}. We need for each k a sum over h≤k of D_h * [ R_{h-1}! * (R_h-C_k)!/R_h! - (R_{h-1}-C_k)! ].
Define X_h = R_{h-1}!, Y_h = R_h! maybe. The term is X_h * (R_h-C_k)!/R_h! - (R_{h-1}-C_k)!.
This is a sum of two terms:
Term1: X_h / R_h! * (R_h-C_k)!.
Term2: - (R_{h-1}-C_k)!.
So Total_k = 1/(R_k-C_k)! [ Σ_{h≤k} D_h * (R_{h-1}!/R_h!) * (R_h-C_k)! - Σ_{h≤k} D_h * (R_{h-1}-C_k)! ].
Let α_h = D_h * R_{h-1}!/R_h!. Then
Total_k = 1/(R_k-C_k)! [ Σ_{h≤k} α_h (R_h-C_k)! - Σ_{h≤k} D_h (R_{h-1}-C_k)! ].
This is a sum of two "factorial difference" convolutions: sequences α_h at positions R_h, and D_h at positions R_{h-1}, evaluated at C_k. Specifically S1_k = Σ_{h≤k} α_h (R_h - C_k)!, S2_k = Σ_{h≤k} D_h (R_{h-1} - C_k)!.
Note R_h > C_k? For h≤k, R_h ≥ R_k ≥ C_k? Since events have c≤r, C_k≤R_k, and R_h≥R_k, so nonnegative. R_{h-1}≥R_h≥C_k.
This is exactly of the form S_i = Σ_{j<i} A[j] F[r_i - c_j] but with r_i = R_h? Actually we need for each k, sum over h≤k of coeff_h * (R_h - C_k)!. Here the first index h has "r" = R_h, and the query c = C_k. Both sequences R_h and C_k are decreasing in reverse (or increasing in forward). If we reverse to forward order, let i = M-h+1, then R_h decreasing becomes R' increasing, C_k decreasing becomes C' increasing. The sum becomes S'_i = Σ_{j≥i?} coeff * (R'_j - C'_i)!? Let's map.

But the expression as two sums of factorial differences suggests we can compute Total_k sequentially using a data structure that supports adding a term α_h at position R_h and querying Σ α_h (R_h - C)!, and adding D_h at position R_{h-1} querying Σ D_h (R_{h-1}-C)!.
